+def levenshtein_dist(s1: str, s2: str) -> int:
+    if len(s1) < len(s2):
+        return levenshtein_dist(s2, s1)
+    if len(s2) == 0:
+        return len(s1)
+    previous_row = range(len(s2) + 1)
+    for i, c1 in enumerate(s1):
+        current_row = [i + 1]
+        for j, c2 in enumerate(s2):
+            insertions = previous_row[j + 1] + 1
+            deletions = current_row[j] + 1
+            substitutions = previous_row[j] + (c1 != c2)
+            current_row.append(min(insertions, de

 * **Parser Synchronization**: Integrated the token scanner inside `buildBlueprint` to look for tokens matching this regex. Project IDs are parsed directly into filters: `{ column: 'project_id', value: upperVal }`.
 * **Fast-Path Bypassing LLM Fallback**: If a `project_id` filter is present, the query is immediately treated as a project details lookup (`hasMetric = true` and `fallback_required = false`). This routes queries to the backend's deterministic "Fast Path" (sub-10ms) without triggering the latency-heavy LLM fallback.

+    return "USD"

+def interpolate_sql(sql_str: str, params: tuple) -> str:
+    if not params:
+        return sql_str
+    parts = sql_str.split('?')
+    interpolated = ""
+    for i, part in enumerate(parts):
+        interpolated += part
+        if i < len(params):
+            val = params[i]
+            if isinstance(val, str):
+                interpolated += f"'{val}'"
+            elif val is None:
+                interpolated += "NULL"
+            else:
+                interpolated += str(val)
+    return interpolated
+
